Traits

Rimed Hide. The dire wolf can pinpoint the location of cold and frost within 30 feet of itself.

Pack Tactics. The dire wolf has Advantage on an attack roll against a creature if at least one of the dire wolf's allies is within 5 feet of the creature and the ally doesn't have the Incapacitated condition.

Actions

Multiattack. The dire wolf makes two Bite attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Chilling Bite.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 8 (1d10 + 3) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Chilling Bite. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target's Speed is halved and it can't take Reactions until the end of the dire wolf's next turn.

Trench Veilsinger

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Neutral

AC 20
Initiative +9 (19)

HP 437 (25d20 + 175)

Speed 40 ft., Swim 40 ft.

MOD

Senses blindsight 120 ft.; Passive Perception 21

Languages Common

CR 24 (XP 62,000; PB +7)

Traits

Amphibious. The veilsinger can breathe air and water.

Fear Aura. The veilsinger emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the veilsinger fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions

Multiattack. The veilsinger makes three Tail attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Drowning Dirge.

Tail. Melee Attack Roll: +16, reach 15 ft. 36 (6d8 + 9) Thunder damage.

Drowning Dirge. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, each creature of the veilsinger's choice in a 120-foot Emanation originating from the veilsinger that can see or hear the veilsinger. Failure: The target has the Charmed condition until the end of its next turn, and while Charmed this way, it must move toward the veilsinger by the safest available route on its turn and can't take Reactions. Success: The target is immune to this creature's lure for 24 hours.

Bonus Actions

Veil of Shadow. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the veilsinger takes the Hide action.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the veilsinger can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Glide Under. The veilsinger moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Tail attack.

Sink From Sight. While in Dim Light or Darkness, the veilsinger takes the Hide action. The veilsinger can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.

Pull Down. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 24, one creature within 30 feet. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The leviathan opens with a subsonic hunting hymn that rolls through the water and unmoors every hull in reach, then falls silent and glides — a darker shape in the trench-dark — until it can bring its mass to bear on the crippled and drowning. Every round after, it circles beneath the wreckage, singing in slow, layered phrases that pull crews over the rails and grind timber apart, and it works a fleet the way a wolf works a herd: splitting, herding, and drowning. Wounded and pressed, it does not flee — it sinks into the abyss, and its song turns from lure to warcry, a killing dirge that rises until the trench itself shakes, then it comes back up to finish what the fleet's guns could not.
[concept] lore: In the black trench-floors beneath the shipping lanes it has sung for longer than any chart remembers, and every fleet ever lost above it was, to the leviathan, merely a meal it pulled down by the sound of its own drowning crews.

```
Bog Hound
Medium beast, unaligned
Armor Class

11 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

22 (3d8 + 9)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


12 (+1)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +3, Perception +2

Saving Throws: 

STR +3

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

None

Challenge

1 (200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+2

Mire Walker. Difficult terrain composed of mud, swamp, and shallow water doesn't cost the bog hound extra movement, and the bog hound can move across mud, swamp, and shallow water without needing to make an ability check.
Whelm. As a bonus action, the bog hound whelms one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While whelmed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the bog hound, and takes 3 (1d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the bog hound's turns. The bog hound can have only one creature whelmed at a time. If the bog hound takes 5 damage or more in a single turn from the whelmed creature, the bog hound must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the bog hound. If the bog hound dies, a whelmed creature pulls free prone by using 5 feet of movement.
Mire Taunt (Bonus Action). One creature within 30 feet must make a DC 13 Wisdom saving throw. On a failed save, until the start of the bog hound's next turn, the target has disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than the bog hound.

Actions
Multiattack: The bog hound makes two Bite attacks.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +3 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 5 (1d8 + 1) piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 11). While grappled, the target is restrained.

```
Death-Knell Sexton

Medium Undead, Neutral

AC 11
Initiative +1 (11)

Speed 5 ft., Fly 40 ft. (hover)

Skills Perception +3, Stealth +5

Resistances Acid, Bludgeoning, Cold, Fire, Lightning, Piercing, Slashing, Thunder

Immunities Necrotic, Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Grappled, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned, Prone, Restrained

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 13

Languages Common plus one other language

CR 3 (XP 700; PB +2)

Traits

Ethereal Sight. The sexton can see 60 feet into the Ethereal Plane when it is on the Material Plane.

Incorporeal Movement. The sexton can move through other creatures and objects as if they were Difficult Terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) Force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.

Actions

Multiattack. The sexton makes two Withering Touch attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Horrific Visage.

Withering Touch. Melee Attack Roll: +5, reach 5 ft. 19 (3d10 + 3) Necrotic damage.

Threshold Execute (1/Day). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, one creature the sexton can see within 60 feet. Failure: If the target has 13 Hit Points or fewer, it has the Unconscious condition for 1 hour, until it takes damage, or until a creature within 5 feet of it takes an action to wake it. Otherwise, it takes 13 (3d8) Necrotic damage.

Horrific Visage. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 60-foot Cone that can see the sexton and isn't an Undead. Failure: 10 (2d6 + 3) Psychic damage, and the target has the Frightened condition until the start of the sexton's next turn. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Horrific Visage for 24 hours.

Possession (Recharge 6). Charisma Saving Throw: DC 13, one Humanoid the sexton can see within 5 feet. Failure: The target is possessed by the sexton; the sexton disappears, and the target has the Incapacitated condition and loses control of its body. The sexton now controls the body, but the target retains awareness. The sexton can't be targeted by any attack, spell, or other effect, except ones that specifically target Undead. The sexton's game statistics are the same, except it uses the possessed target's Speed, as well as the target's Strength, Dexterity, and Constitution modifiers.
The possession lasts until the body drops to 0 Hit Points or the sexton leaves as a Bonus Action. When the possession ends, the sexton appears in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the target, and the target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours. Success: The target is immune to this ghost's Possession for 24 hours.

Etherealness. The sexton casts the Etherealness spell, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability. The sexton is visible on the Material Plane while on the Border Ethereal and vice versa, but it can't affect or be affected by anything on the other plane.

Every round after that it scrambles up rock, wall, or canopy to peel a single straggler away, using its climbing limbs to attack from angles a grounded pack cannot cover. When bloodied it does not flee but climbs out of reach and circles, shrieking and waiting for the herd to split before dropping again.
[concept] lore: Born in the cavern-riddled foothills where cliff faces meet old forest, this owlbear learned that the third dimension is a hunting ground, and now it treats any wall, ledge, or ship's mast as a road to unguarded backs.

## Screedrift — a homebrew with a nova

**Concept:** a quarry-pit thing of living scree that buries what it catches

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Screedrift [seed 1/2] | 5 (2014) | brute-5-grappler | ok | SOFT: band | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok | ok |

- bands · soft · band: reads CR 3.5 (print at 5 reads 2.5–9.5, n=25)
- read: def 2 / off 5 → 3.5; sustained 32 (2 × Avalanche Slam); nova Erupting Ambush 36; dmg 33

```
Screedrift
Large elemental, neutral
Armor Class

13 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

93 (11d10 + 33)

Speed: 

30 ft., burrow 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

8 (-1)

	

16 (+3)

	

4 (-3)

	

10 (+0)

	

4 (-3)

Skills: 

Athletics +6, Perception +3

Saving Throws: 

STR +6

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

tremorsense 60 ft., passive Perception 13

Languages: 

None

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Scree Slide. Difficult terrain composed of rubble, broken stone, or excavated ground doesn't cost the screedrift extra movement, and the screedrift can move across rubble, broken stone, or excavated ground without needing to make an ability check.
Entomb. As a bonus action, the screedrift entombs one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While entombed, the target is blinded and restrained, has total cover against attacks and other effects outside the screedrift, and takes 7 (2d6) bludgeoning damage at the start of each of the screedrift's turns. The screedrift can have only one creature entombed at a time. If the screedrift takes 15 damage or more in a single turn from the entombed creature, the screedrift must release the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits prone in an unoccupied space within 5 feet of the screedrift. If the screedrift dies, an entombed creature breaks free prone by using 5 feet of movement.

Actions
Multiattack: The screedrift makes two Avalanche Slam attacks.
Avalanche Slam: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 14). While grappled, the target is restrained.
Erupting Ambush (Recharge 5-6): If the screedrift is burrowing, it erupts from the ground at a point within 5 feet of one creature. That creature must make a DC 14 Dexterity saving throw, taking 36 (8d8) bludgeoning damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also knocked prone. The screedrift then makes one Avalanche Slam attack against the target.
```

Once it has a victim pinned, it grinds over them each round, loosing more scree to keep them buried while it shifts its bulk to fend off rescuers. It has no fear and no mercy — it simply keeps piling stone until the struggling stops.
[concept] lore: When a quarry was abandoned mid-dig, the spilled stone and the crushed dead beneath it woke as one hungry drift, and it now drags anything that walks the pit floor down under its own weight.

## Vampire — a legendary

**Concept:** name: Vampire | who keeps a garden of thorns

```
Vampire
Medium undead, lawful evil
Armor Class

16 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

195 (23d8 + 92)

Speed: 

40 ft., climb 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

15 (+2)

	

18 (+4)

Skills: 

Perception +7, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CHA +9

Damage Resistances: 

necrotic

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

exhaustion, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 17

Languages: 

Common plus two other languages

Challenge

13 (10,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+5

Thorn Garden. The vampire keeps a garden of thorns within 30 feet of itself. That object has AC 17, 25 hit points, and resistance to all damage. While it is intact, the vampire regains 20 hit points at the start of each of its turns.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the vampire fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Misty Escape. If the vampire drops to 0 hit points outside its resting place, the vampire uses Shape-Shift to become mist (no action required). If it can't use Shape-Shift, it is destroyed.
While it has 0 hit points in mist form, it can't return to its vampire form, and it must reach its resting place within 2 hours or be destroyed. Once in its resting place, it returns to its vampire form and is paralyzed until it regains any hit points, and it regains 1 hit point after spending 1 hour there.
Spider Climb. The vampire can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.
Vampire Weakness. The vampire has these weaknesses: Forbiddance: the vampire can't enter a residence without an invitation from an occupant.
Running Water: the vampire takes 20 acid damage if it ends its turn in running water.
Stake to the Heart: If a weapon that deals piercing damage is driven into the vampire's heart while the vampire is incapacitated in its resting place, the vampire is paralyzed until the weapon is removed.
Sunlight: the vampire takes 20 radiant damage if it starts its turn in sunlight. While in sunlight, it has disadvantage on attack rolls and ability checks.
Shape-Shift. As a bonus action, if the vampire isn't in sunlight or running water, it shape-shifts into a Tiny bat (speed 5 ft., fly speed 30 ft.) or a Medium cloud of mist (speed 5 ft., fly speed 20 ft. [hover]), or it returns to its vampire form. Anything it is wearing transforms with it.
While in bat form, the vampire can't speak. Its game statistics, other than its size and speed, are unchanged.
While in mist form, the vampire can't take any actions, speak, or manipulate objects. It is weightless and can enter an enemy's space and stop there. If air can pass through a space, the mist can do so, but it can't pass through liquid. It has resistance to all damage, except the damage it takes from sunlight.
Charm (Recharge 5-6). As a bonus action, the vampire casts charm person, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17), and the duration is 24 hours. The charmed target is a willing recipient of the vampire's Bite, the damage of which doesn't end the spell. When the spell ends, the target is unaware it was charmed by the vampire.

Actions
Multiattack: The vampire makes two Grave Strike attacks and uses Bite. It can replace one attack with a use of Thorned Snare.
Grave Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) {energy_type} damage, plus 7 (2d6) necrotic damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it is grappled (escape DC 17) by one of two hands.
Thorned Snare: One creature within 60 feet of the vampire must succeed on a DC 17 Dexterity saving throw or be restrained until the end of its next turn.
Bite (Bat or Vampire Form Only): One creature within 5 feet of the vampire that is willing or that is grappled, incapacitated, or restrained must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 6 (1d4 + 4) piercing damage plus 13 (3d8) necrotic damage on a failed save. The target's hit point maximum decreases by an amount equal to the necrotic damage taken, and the vampire regains hit points equal to that amount. A Humanoid reduced to 0 hit points by this damage and then buried rises the following sunset as a Vampire Spawn under the vampire's control.
Legendary Actions

The vampire can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The vampire regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathless Strike: The vampire moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Grave Strike attack.
Beguile: The vampire casts command, requiring no spell components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17). The vampire can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

[concept] identity: A pale gardener-vampire drifts through its bramble maze with its thorned tools, opening every hunt with a silent strike from the black growth — rooting the victim with a thrust of ironwood into the deep shade. Each round after, it withdraws into the hedges, bleeding the wound with clinging thorns and shadow, and lets the garden do the rest. Cornered and bloodied, it does not flee; it lifts the whole tangle of briar and bone around itself and fights like a plant defending its soil, patient and terrible to the last.
[concept] lore: Once a lord who fed his roses on the blood of trespassers, it was staked for the crime and now rises in the garden it made — wanting only for the hedge to grow tall enough to hide the sun forever.

Huge elemental, neutral

Grinding March. Difficult terrain composed of ice, snow, rubble, or broken ground doesn't cost the glacier heart extra movement, and the glacier heart can move across ice, snow, rubble, or broken ground without needing to make an ability check.
Cold Absorption. Whenever the glacier heart is subjected to cold damage, it takes no damage and instead regains a number of hit points equal to the cold damage dealt.
Siege Monster. The glacier heart deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions
Multiattack: The glacier heart makes two Icebound Fist attacks.
Icebound Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +9 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 22 (4d8 + 4) bludgeoning damage plus 13 (3d8) cold damage.
Killing Cold (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 30-foot cone must make a DC 17 Constitution saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) cold damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. On a failed save, the target is also deafened until the end of its next turn.

[concept] identity: It advances in a slow, crushing stride, its granite limbs grinding and its glacial heart bleeding a frozen wake that lingers long after it has passed, and it meets every charge by walking through it — through shield walls, through fortifications, through the fallen — until the cold behind it has done its work. Every round it simply closes ground, and prey who flee only find that the killing cold it leaves has already taken the paths behind them. It feels no pain, no fear, and no hurry; it will not break, will not bleed, and will not stop until the heart that drives it is shattered.
[concept] lore: Carved from the living core of a glacier by some hand that wanted a weapon that could not tire, the heart walks still on legs of packed ice and stone, and it wants only to return to the cold that made it — by making all ground as cold as itself.

Huge Construct, Lawful Evil

Immunities Poison; Charmed, Exhaustion, Frightened, Paralyzed, Petrified, Poisoned

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 16

Languages Common

CR 17 (XP 18,000; PB +6)

Traits

Fear Aura. The tyrant emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 19, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the end of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the tyrant fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Immutable Form. The tyrant can't shape-shift.

Siege Monster. The tyrant deals double damage to objects and structures.

Actions

Multiattack. The tyrant makes two Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Opportunist's Lash.

Fist. Melee Attack Roll: +12, reach 10 ft. 20 (4d6 + 6) Bludgeoning damage plus 14 (4d6) Fire damage.

Furnace Blast (Recharge 5-6). Constitution Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature in a 30-foot Cone. Failure: 72 (16d8) Fire damage. Success: Half damage only.

Opportunist's Lash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, one creature within 30 feet that is Prone. Failure: 59 (17d6) Fire damage. Success: Half damage.

Legendary Actions

Legendary Action Uses: 3. Immediately after another creature's turn, the tyrant can expend a use to take one of the following actions. It regains all expended uses at the start of each of its turns.

Trample Through. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks, and it makes one Fist attack.

Breach Walls. The tyrant moves up to half its Speed without provoking Opportunity Attacks. During this movement, it can move through Large or smaller objects and structures, destroying them.

Crushing Step. Strength Saving Throw: DC 19, each creature within 10 feet of the tyrant. Failure: The target has the Prone condition.

Chrism Vault Ooze
Large ooze, unaligned
Armor Class

10 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

153 (18d10 + 54)

Speed: 

20 ft., climb 20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


16 (+3)

	

6 (-2)

	

16 (+3)

	

2 (-4)

	

8 (-1)

	

1 (-5)

Damage Immunities: 

acid

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, deafened, exhaustion, frightened, grappled, prone, restrained

Senses: 

blindsight 30 ft., passive Perception 9

Languages: 

None

Challenge

9 (5,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Relic Nourishment. The vault ooze regains 10 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point and a reliquary, shrine, anointed vessel, or holy relic is within 30 feet of it.
Amorphous. The vault ooze can move through a space as narrow as 1 inch wide without squeezing.
Corrosive Form. A creature that touches the vault ooze or hits it with a melee attack while within 5 feet of it takes 9 (2d8) acid damage.
Spider Climb. The vault ooze can climb difficult surfaces, including along ceilings, without needing to make an ability check.

Actions
Multiattack: The vault ooze makes two Pseudopod attacks.
Pseudopod: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 16 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage plus 11 (2d10) acid damage.
```

Any enemy that starts its turn in the aura must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened of Brannoc until the end of its next turn. On a successful save, the target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.
Retaliating Slam. As a bonus action, Brannoc targets one creature within 5 feet of it that hit it with an attack roll since the end of Brannoc's last turn. The target takes 11 (2d10) bludgeoning damage.

Actions
Multiattack: Brannoc makes two attacks: one with Maul and one with Masonry.

Masonry: Ranged Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, range 60/240 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.

Krennik Skullstandard
Medium humanoid, neutral evil
Armor Class

14 (leather)

Hit Points: 

78 (12d8 + 24)

50 ft.

passive Perception 13

+2

Blood Frenzy. Krennik has advantage on attack rolls against any creature that doesn't have all its hit points.
Caracole. Krennik ignores difficult terrain, and opportunity attacks against it are made with disadvantage.
Mounted Agility. As a bonus action, Krennik takes the Dash or Disengage action.

Actions
Multiattack: Krennik makes two attacks: one with Saber and one with Javelin. It can replace one attack with a use of Opportunist's Lash.
Saber: Melee Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (2d6 + 3) slashing damage. If Krennik moved at least 20 feet in a straight line toward the target immediately before the hit, the target takes an extra 4 (1d8) slashing damage and, if it is Medium or smaller, is knocked prone.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +5 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 7 (1d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Galloping Charge (Recharge 5-6): Krennik moves up to its speed without provoking opportunity attacks and can move through the spaces of Medium or smaller creatures. Each creature whose space it enters must make a DC 13 Dexterity saving throw, taking 24 (7d6) bludgeoning damage on a failed save and being knocked prone.
Opportunist's Lash: One creature within 30 feet of Krennik that is prone must make a DC 13 Strength saving throw, taking 17 (5d6) piercing damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
```

Actions

Multiattack. The air elemental makes two Thunderous Slam attacks.

Whirlwind (Recharge 4-6). Strength Saving Throw: DC 13, one Medium or smaller creature in the air elemental's space. Failure: 24 (4d10 + 2) Thunder damage, and the target is pushed up to 20 feet straight away from the air elemental and has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

Hydra
Huge monstrosity, unaligned
Armor Class

15 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

184 (16d12 + 80)

Speed: 

40 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


20 (+5)

	

12 (+1)

	

20 (+5)

	

2 (-4)

	

10 (+0)

	

7 (-2)

Perception +6

fire

blinded, charmed, deafened, frightened, stunned, unconscious

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 16

Languages: 

None

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Hold Breath. The hydra can hold its breath for 1 hour.
Multiple Heads. The hydra has five heads. Whenever the hydra takes 25 damage or more on a single turn, one of its heads dies. The hydra dies if all its heads are dead. At the end of each of its turns when it has at least one living head, the hydra grows two heads for each of its heads that died since its last turn, unless it has taken fire damage since its last turn. The hydra regains 20 hit points when it grows new heads.
Reactive Heads. For each head the hydra has beyond one, it gets an extra reaction that can be used only for opportunity attacks.

Actions
Multiattack: The hydra makes as many Bite attacks as it has heads. It can replace one attack with a use of Choking Stump Smoke.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 10 (1d10 + 5) piercing damage.
Choking Stump Smoke: One creature within 60 feet of the hydra must succeed on a DC 16 Constitution saving throw or be blinded until the start of its next turn.

[concept] identity: The Hydra closes on the nearest foe and lashes with its many heads to seize and pin, trusting a reknitting hide to shrug off whatever the prey answers with. Every round after that it tears at grappled victims while its severed stumps pour scalding smoke that chokes and blinds anyone in reach — the wounds are closing, but the smoking stump is its real weapon. It never retreats to save a neck; it fights to the death with an old serpent's patience, letting a cut neck bleed smoke over the whole fight.
[concept] lore: A many-headed serpent of the waste whose blood runs thick with a scalding, hardening resin, so that every neck it loses becomes a smoldering vented stump that smokes out its hunters rather than a new head.

Immutable Form. The ramble can't shape-shift.

Magic Resistance. The ramble has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The ramble makes two Iron Fist attacks.

Each creature whose space it enters is targeted once by the following effect. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 16. Failure: 45 (13d6) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage only.

[concept] identity: A ramshackle siege-engine golem of lashed and pegged timber that opens every fight by charging the nearest wall, gate, or shield-line and shouldering straight through it in a hail of splinters and masonry, scattering whoever stood behind it. Every round after that it walks a straight line at the biggest structure or the densest cluster and repeats the breach, battering with beam-fists and hurling the wreckage it has just made, and it never breaks off to chase a lone skirmisher. It fights to total ruin: pressure only makes it pick up speed, and a bloodied siege-golem simply finds a thicker wall.
[concept] lore: Built by a forgotten army from scaffolds, battering beams, and wagon-axles bound with raw hide rope, the engine outlived its war and still walks toward the last fortress it was ordered to flatten, breaching anything in the line between.

[concept] identity: A carved saint of weeping limestone, it advances at a pilgrim's unhurried pace until it is among its foes, then brings down both fists in slow, crushing arcs that crater the ground where they land. Every round it chooses the nearest person who has raised a hand against it and walks through walls, doors, and furniture to reach them, weeping runnels of mineral water that pool and harden at its feet. It never flees and never hurries; when bloodied it does not rage but simply keeps swinging, patient and sorrowful, until nothing moves in front of it.
[concept] lore: A cathedral's grief made stone, this saint was carved to intercede for the dead and rose when its prayers went unanswered, and it now walks the world that abandoned it looking for the one worshipper it was promised.

Drowned Cathedral Choir
Large undead, neutral
Armor Class

14 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

178 (21d10 + 63)

Speed: 

5 ft., fly 60 ft. (hover), swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


6 (-2)

	

16 (+3)

	

17 (+3)

	

12 (+1)

	

14 (+2)

	

15 (+2)

Skills: 

Stealth +7

DEX +7

Damage Resistances: 

bludgeoning, piercing, slashing

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, grappled, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned, prone, restrained

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 12

Languages: 

Common plus the languages it knew in life

Challenge

10 (5,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Fear Aura. The choir emanates an aura in a 20-foot-radius sphere while it is not incapacitated. Any enemy that starts its turn in the aura must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or be frightened until the start of its next turn. A creature that succeeds on this saving throw is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.
Incorporeal Movement. The choir can move through other creatures and objects as if they were difficult terrain. It takes 5 (1d10) force damage if it ends its turn inside an object.
Sunlight Sensitivity. While in sunlight, the choir has disadvantage on ability checks and attack rolls.

Actions
Multiattack: The choir makes two Hymn attacks.
Hymn: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 30 (6d8 + 3) necrotic damage.

[concept] identity: The Drowned Cathedral Choir opens a fight not by striking but by singing — a single harmonized note that rolls through walls and pulls the warmth from living lungs, a drowned hymn that drains the living and leaves them staggering. Every round after, it drifts through stone and flesh alike, never breaking formation or its one terrible voice, harrying prey with its dirge and rising in volume as more voices join the chord. It is relentless and patient, refusing to flee even when bloodied — a thing of the grave that has already drowned and cannot die again, and it will sing a victim down until the last breath leaves them.
[concept] lore: When a great cathedral sank beneath black water with its choir still singing, the flood took the bodies but the hymn clung to the souls, and the drowned choir now drifts through drowned naves and the walls of the living world, seeking new throats to swell its endless verse.

Purple Worm

Gargantuan Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 18
Initiative -2 (8)

HP 247 (15d20 + 90)

Speed 50 ft., Burrow 50 ft.

Skills Athletics +14, Perception +4

Senses blindsight 30 ft., tremorsense 60 ft.; Passive Perception 14

Languages None

CR 15 (XP 13,000; PB +5)

Traits

Weapon Lodge. When a creature hits the purple worm with a nonmagical weapon, the weapon lodges in the purple worm's body. A lodged weapon can be pulled free by a creature within reach that succeeds on a DC 21 Strength (Athletics) check as an action; while lodged, the purple worm can use it: its Bite attacks deal an extra 22 (5d8) Acid damage per lodged weapon (maximum two).

Tunneler. The purple worm can burrow through solid rock at half its Burrow Speed and leaves a 10-foot-diameter tunnel in its wake.

Actions

Multiattack. The purple worm makes one Bite attack and one Tail Stinger attack. It can replace one attack with a use of Gullet Drag.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 22 (3d8 + 9) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22), and it has the Restrained condition until the grapple ends.

Tail Stinger. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 16 (2d6 + 9) Piercing damage plus 35 (10d6) Poison damage.

Gullet Drag. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Huge or smaller creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is pulled up to 30 feet straight toward the purple worm by its grinding gullet and has the Grappled condition (escape DC 22). While Grappled this way,

Bonus Actions

Swallow. Strength Saving Throw: DC 21, one Large or smaller creature Grappled by the purple worm (it can have up to three creatures swallowed at a time). Failure: The target is swallowed by the purple worm, and the Grappled condition ends. A swallowed creature has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the purple worm, and takes 17 (5d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the purple worm's turns.
If the purple worm takes 30 damage or more on a single turn from a creature inside it, the purple worm must succeed on a DC 21 Constitution saving throw at the end of that turn or regurgitate all swallowed creatures, each of which falls in a space within 5 feet of the purple worm and has the Prone condition. If the purple worm dies, any swallowed creature no longer has the Restrained condition and can escape from the corpse using 20 feet of movement, exiting Prone.

Every round it lunges and engulfs again, spitting the half-crushed husk of armor from a maw rimmed in jagged, swallowed iron, and a battered band that wounds it only feels it thrash harder, wedging more lost arms into its coiled hide. It never flees; once bloodied it devours with a mindless, sated focus until the last prey is scrap in its belly.
[concept] lore: Deep in old war-torn badlands, a purple worm swallowed an entire routed war-band's baggage train and grew around the loot, and now it surfaces wherever armies march, hungry for fresh armor to feed the furnace within.

Perception +13, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6, WIS +7

Damage Immunities: 

fire

Senses: 

blindsight 60 ft., darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 23

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

17 (18,000 XP)

+6

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the red dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Sight Weakness. The red dragon has disadvantage on Wisdom (Perception) checks that rely on sight.

Actions
Multiattack: The red dragon makes three Rend attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Spellcasting to cast Scorching Ray.
Rend: Melee Weapon Attack: +14 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 13 (1d10 + 8) slashing damage plus 5 (2d4) fire damage.
Spellcasting: The red dragon casts one of the following spells, requiring no material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20, +12 to hit with spell attacks): -

At will: command, detect magic, scorching ray

1/day each: fireball
Wing Buffet: One creature within 5 feet of the red dragon that it can see must succeed on a DC 21 Strength saving throw or take 13 (3d8) bludgeoning damage, and if the target is Medium or smaller, it is knocked prone.
Fire Breath (Recharge 5-6): Each creature in a 60-foot cone must make a DC 21 Dexterity saving throw, taking 59 (17d6) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Legendary Actions

The red dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The red dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Commanding Presence: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast command (cast at 2nd level). The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Fiery Rays: The red dragon uses Spellcasting to cast scorching ray. The red dragon can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Pounce: The red dragon moves up to half its speed, and it makes one Rend attack.

[concept] identity: A vast red dragon who no longer bothers to duel: it opens by scalding a stretch of ground with its breath, then commits hard down the one side its ruined eye still tracks, angling to keep prey inside that blind-spot half-circle so it can never be flanked cleanly again. It fights on its terms with claw, bite and wingbeat, and its temper is a broken thing — wounded once by a lance, it kills anything that crowds its dead side, and it will not flee a fight it believes it is winning. Under real pressure it climbs into the air and turns the ground beneath it to slag rather than disengage, punishing the field itself.
[concept] lore: Lanced through the eye in a war it barely remembers, this old adult red carried the weapon's shaft with it as a grudge and now holds a scorched mountain reach as its hoard-vault, hunting by smell and heat rather than sight.

Pit Fiend

Initiative +2 (12)

Languages Infernal; telepathy 120 ft.

CR 20 (XP 25,000; PB +6)

Traits

Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the pit fiend fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Diabolical Restoration. If the pit fiend dies outside the Nine Hells, its body disappears in sulfurous smoke, and it gains a new body instantly, reviving with all its Hit Points somewhere in the Nine Hells.

Fear Aura. The pit fiend emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 21, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this pit fiend's aura for 24 hours.

Magic Resistance. The pit fiend has Advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.

Actions

Multiattack. The pit fiend makes one Bite attack, two Devilish Claw attacks, and one Fiery Mace attack. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Merciless Strike or (B) Spellcasting to cast Bestow Curse.

Bite. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 18 (3d6 + 8) Piercing damage. If the target is a creature, it must make the following saving throw. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21. Failure: The target has the Poisoned condition. While Poisoned, the target can't regain Hit Points and takes 21 (6d6) Poison damage at the start of each of its turns, and it repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 1 minute, it succeeds automatically.

Devilish Claw. Melee Attack Roll: +14, reach 10 ft. 26 (4d8 + 8) Necrotic damage.

Spellcasting. The pit fiend casts one of the following spells, requiring no Material components and using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 20):

- At Will: Bestow Curse, Hold Person, Fear
- 1/Day Each: Wall of Fire (level 5 version)

Merciless Strike. The pit fiend speaks the oath back at it. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 21, one creature within 60 feet that has the Frightened condition. Failure: 58 (13d8) Fire damage, and the target has the Prone condition. Success: Half damage.

Hellfire Spellcasting (Recharge 4-6). The pit fiend casts Fireball (level 5 version) twice, requiring no Material components and using Charisma as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 21). It can replace one Fireball with Hold Monster (level 7 version) or Wall of Fire.

[concept] identity: A pit fiend of oath-keeping circles dying knights in its binding rings and catches the last sworn word before the soul slips free, sealing each vow into the iron of its hide. It opens by cursing the loudest foe into a name-bound silence, then spends every round stripping actions — freezing a charging knight mid-stride, turning an ally's blade aside with a single spoken term of the old contract. It does not flee and does not finish quickly; it lets the wounded run a few steps, then calls their own oath back at them to drag them down.
[concept] lore: Forged in the lowest courts of the Hells, the oath-collector was made to hold what mortals swear at the moment they die, and it drags every broken promise back down as evidence against its debtor.

```
Sunbone Archlich
Medium undead, neutral evil
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

315 (42d8 + 126)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


11 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

16 (+3)

	

21 (+5)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +17, History +11, Insight +8, Perception +8

Saving Throws: 

CON +9, INT +11, WIS +8

Damage Resistances: 

cold, lightning

Damage Immunities: 

necrotic, poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 120 ft., passive Perception 18

Languages: 

All

Challenge

20 (25,000 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+6

Sunbone Orbit. The archlich keeps its bone sun within 30 feet of itself. That object has AC 17, 25 hit points, and resistance to all damage. While it is intact, the archlich regains 20 hit points at the start of each of its turns.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the archlich fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.
Spirit Jar. If destroyed, the archlich reforms in 1d10 days if it has a spirit jar, reviving with all its hit points. The new body appears in an unoccupied space within the archlich's lair.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When the archlich is hit by an attack, the archlich casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: The archlich makes three attacks, using Eldritch Burst or Paralyzing Touch. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) or (B) Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness.
Eldritch Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft., one target. Hit: 31 (4d12 + 5) force damage.
Paralyzing Touch: Melee Spell Attack: +11 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 15 (3d6 + 5) cold damage, and the target must succeed on a DC 19 Constitution saving throw or be paralyzed until the start of the archlich's next turn.
Spellcasting: The archlich casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 19): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, dispel magic, fireball, invisibility, lightning bolt, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: animate dead, dimension door, plane shift

1/day each: chain lightning, finger of death, power word kill, scrying
Legendary Actions

The archlich can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The archlich regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Deathly Teleport: The archlich teleports up to 60 feet to an unoccupied space it can see, and each creature within 10 feet of the space it left takes 11 (2d10) necrotic damage.
Disrupt Life: Each creature that isn't an undead within 20 feet of the archlich must make a DC 19 Constitution saving throw, taking 31 (9d6) necrotic damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one. Whether it fails or succeeds, the archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
Frightening Gaze: The archlich casts fear, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting. The archlich can't take this action again until the start of its next turn.
```

```
Ancient Gold Dragon
Gargantuan dragon, lawful good
Armor Class

22 (Natural Armor)

Hit Points: 

546 (28d20 + 252)

Speed: 

40 ft., fly 80 ft., swim 40 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


30 (+10)

	

14 (+2)

	

29 (+9)

	

18 (+4)

	

17 (+3)

	

28 (+9)

Skills: 

Insight +10, Perception +17, Persuasion +16, Stealth +9

Saving Throws: 

DEX +9, CON +16, WIS +10, CHA +16

Damage Immunities: 

Fire

Senses: 

Blindsight 60 ft., Darkvision 120 ft., Passive Perception 27

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

24

Proficiency Bonus:

+7

Amphibious. The dragon can breathe air and water.
Legendary Resistance (3/Day). If the dragon fails a saving throw, it can choose to succeed instead.

Actions
Multiattack: The dragon can use its Frightful Presence. It then makes three attacks: one with its bite and two with its claws.
Bite: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 15 ft., one target. Hit: 21 (2d10 + 10) piercing damage.
Claw: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (2d6 + 10) slashing damage.
Tail: Melee Weapon Attack: +17 to hit, reach 20 ft., one target. Hit: 19 (2d8 + 10) bludgeoning damage.
Frightful Presence: Each creature of the dragon's choice that is within 120 feet of the dragon and aware of it must succeed on a DC 24 Wisdom saving throw or become frightened for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. If a creature's saving throw is successful or the effect ends for it, the creature is immune to the dragon's Frightful Presence for the next 24 hours.
Breath Weapons (Recharge 5–6): The dragon uses one of the following breath weapons.
Fire Breath: The dragon exhales fire in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must make a DC 24 Dexterity saving throw, taking 71 (13d10) fire damage on a failed save, or half as much damage on a successful one.
Weakening Breath: The dragon exhales gas in a 90-foot cone. Each creature in that area must succeed on a DC 24 Strength saving throw or have disadvantage on Strength-based attack rolls, Strength checks, and Strength saving throws for 1 minute. A creature can repeat the saving throw at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success.
Change Shape: The dragon magically polymorphs into a humanoid or beast that has a challenge rating no higher than its own, or back into its true form. It reverts to its true form if it dies. Any equipment it is wearing or carrying is absorbed or borne by the new form (the dragon's choice).

In a new form, the dragon retains its alignment, hit points, Hit Dice, ability to speak, proficiencies, Legendary Resistance, lair actions, and Intelligence, Wisdom, and Charisma scores, as well as this action. Its statistics and capabilities are otherwise replaced by those of the new form, except any class features or legendary actions of that form.
Legendary Actions

The ancient gold dragon can take 3 legendary actions, choosing from the options below. Only one legendary action option can be used at a time and only at the end of another creature's turn. The ancient gold dragon regains spent legendary actions at the start of its turn.

Detect: The dragon makes a Wisdom (Perception) check.
Tail Attack: The dragon makes a tail attack.
Wing Attack (Costs 2 Actions): The dragon beats its wings. Each creature within 15 feet of the dragon must succeed on a DC 25 Dexterity saving throw or take 17 (2d6 + 10) bludgeoning damage and be knocked prone. The dragon can then fly up to half its flying speed.
```

CR 6 (XP 2,300; PB +3)

Traits

Unbroken Formation. While an ally within 30 feet of Vashka is subject to an effect that would move it or knock it Prone, That ally can't be moved against its will or has the Prone condition by the effect, as Vashka holds the line.

Actions

Multiattack. Vashka makes three Longsword attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shield Bash.

Longsword. Melee Attack Roll: +7, reach 5 ft. 11 (2d6 + 4) Slashing damage.

Shield Bash. Strength Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 5 feet that Vashka can see. Failure: 9 (2d4 + 4) Bludgeoning damage. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it has the Prone condition.

Bonus Actions

Formation Command (2/Day). Each ally within 30 feet of Vashka gains 11 (2d10) Temporary Hit Points.

Reactions

Riposte. Trigger: A creature within 5 feet of Vashka misses it with a melee attack roll. Response: Vashka makes one Longsword attack against that creature.

## Scythebrood Ravener — in the run

**Concept:** A Large insectile horror. It has four scythe-arms, a spitting maw, and a shell that sheds acid. It fights by pinning prey with two arms and carving with the others.

```
Scythebrood Ravener

Large Monstrosity, Unaligned

AC 15
Initiative -1 (9)

HP 161 (19d10 + 57)

Speed 40 ft., Climb 40 ft.

Skills Athletics +8, Perception +5

Senses darkvision 60 ft.; Passive Perception 15

Languages None

CR 9 (XP 5,000; PB +4)

Traits

Four Scythe-Arms. The ravener has four scythe-arms; while it has a creature Grappled, it can still make a Spitting Maw attack against another creature.

Actions

Multiattack. The ravener makes two attacks, using Spitting Maw or Scythe-Arm Pin in any combination. It can replace one attack with a use of Merciless Strike.

Spitting Maw. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 10 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16).

Merciless Strike. The ravener carves the seams where shell meets flesh. Constitution Saving Throw: DC 15, one creature within 30 feet that has the Grappled condition. Failure: 63 (14d8) Acid damage. Success: Half damage.

Scythe-Arm Pin. Melee Attack Roll: +8, reach 5 ft. 31 (6d8 + 4) Piercing damage. If the target is a Large or smaller creature, it has the Grappled condition (escape DC 16).

Bonus Actions

Swallow. The ravener swallows one Medium or smaller creature it is grappling. While swallowed, the target has the Blinded and Restrained conditions, has Total Cover against attacks and other effects outside the ravener, and takes 10 (3d6) Acid damage at the start of each of the ravener's turns. The ravener can have only one creature swallowed at a time. If the ravener takes 25 damage or more on a single turn from the swallowed creature, the ravener must regurgitate the target at the end of that turn, and the target exits Prone in an unoccupied space within 10 feet of the ravener. If the ravener dies, a swallowed creature exits the body Prone by using 10 feet of movement.

## Black Iron Sentinel — in the run

**Concept:** a sentinel of black iron, AC 20, Huge, speed 20 feet

| block | CR | donor | derivation | bands | structure | gates | headerBody | edition | donor | adjudicability | reader | class |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

- clean
- read: def 10 / off 8 → 9; sustained 50 (2 × Iron Fist); nova none; dmg 50

```
Black Iron Sentinel
Huge construct, lawful neutral
Armor Class

20 (natural armor)

Hit Points: 

168 (16d12 + 64)

Speed: 

20 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

8 (-1)

	

18 (+4)

	

7 (-2)

	

10 (+0)

	

3 (-4)

Skills: 

Perception +4

Saving Throws: 

CON +8

Damage Immunities: 

poison

Condition Immunities: 

charmed, exhaustion, frightened, paralyzed, petrified, poisoned

Senses: 

darkvision 60 ft., passive Perception 14

Languages: 

understands the languages of its creator but can't speak

Challenge

11 (7,200 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Ward Bond. The iron sentinel is magically bound to a ward of its choosing, and it can have only one ward at a time. While the iron sentinel is within 60 feet of its ward, half of any damage the ward takes (round up) is transferred to the iron sentinel.
Regeneration. The iron sentinel regains 10 hit points at the start of each of its turns if it has at least 1 hit point.
Interpose (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of the iron sentinel that it guards is hit by an attack, the attack hits the iron sentinel instead, and the iron sentinel has resistance to the attack's damage.

Actions
Multiattack: The iron sentinel makes two Iron Fist attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shadow Lurch.
Iron Fist: Melee Weapon Attack: +8 to hit, reach 10 ft., one target. Hit: 25 (6d6 + 4) bludgeoning damage.
Iron Ward (2/Day): The iron sentinel targets itself or one creature it can see within 60 feet. The target gains 24 (7d6) temporary hit points, and it gains a +2 bonus to AC until the end of the iron sentinel's next turn.
Shadow Lurch: Each creature in a 30-foot cone must succeed on a DC 16 Strength saving throw or be pushed up to 15 feet away from the iron sentinel and be knocked prone.
```

[concept] identity: A sentinel of black iron advances as an implacable wall of riveted plate, forcing itself between its charge and any threat before the enemy can close. Each round it hammers with a massive iron limb and body-checks foes off their line, anchoring itself to the ground so nothing can move it or what it guards. It never routs and never toys with prey—iron holds until it is broken, so it fights to the death for as long as a single rivet stands.
[concept] lore: Forge-bound to a long-dead oath, the Black Iron Sentinel stands in a lightless vault or at the head of an old road, still guarding a charge whose name it can no longer speak.

Brinewrack Midwife

Medium Humanoid, Neutral

AC 15
Initiative +2 (12)

HP 81 (18d8)

Speed 30 ft., Swim 30 ft.

MOD

STR
9

14

11

+3

Skills Arcana +6, History +6, Perception +4

Resistances Cold

Senses Passive Perception 14

Languages Common and any three languages, Sylvan

CR 7 (XP 2,900; PB +3)

Actions

Multiattack. The midwife makes three Arcane Burst attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of (A) Retributive Hex or (B) Spellcasting to cast Ray of Sickness.

Arcane Burst. Melee or Ranged Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. or range 120 ft. 16 (3d8 + 3) Force damage.

Spellcasting. The midwife casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 14):

- At Will: Detect Magic, Light, Mage Armor, Mage Hand, Prestidigitation
- 2/Day Each: Fireball, Invisibility
- 1/Day Each: Cone of Cold, Fly

Retributive Hex. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: The target is cursed until the end of the midwife's next turn. The first time the cursed target deals damage to any creature before then, it takes 10 (3d6) Necrotic damage.

Trespasser's Curse. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 14, one creature within 60 feet. Failure: 19 (3d12) Necrotic damage.

Bonus Actions

Misty Step (3/Day). The midwife casts Misty Step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Reactions

Protective Magic (3/Day). The midwife casts Counterspell or Shield in response to the spell's trigger, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.

Ilvareth Dusksong
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

17 (splint)

Hit Points: 

170 (31d8 + 31)

Speed: 

30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

14 (+2)

	

12 (+1)

	

20 (+5)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Arcana +13, History +9, Perception +6

Saving Throws: 

INT +9, WIS +6

Damage Immunities: 

psychic, charmed ((with mind blank))

Senses: 

passive Perception 16

Languages: 

Common plus five other languages, Elvish, Undercommon

Challenge

12 (8,400 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+4

Bound. Ilvareth is bound to its resting place. It can't willingly move more than 120 feet from its resting place, and it always knows the direction and distance to its resting place.
Magic Resistance. Ilvareth has advantage on saving throws against spells and other magical effects.
Shadow Step (2/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth teleports up to 30 feet to an unoccupied space it can see.
Misty Step (3/Day). As a bonus action, Ilvareth casts misty step, using the same spellcasting ability as Spellcasting.
Protective Magic (3/Day) (Reaction). When Ilvareth is hit by an attack roll, Ilvareth casts shield in response, gaining a +5 bonus to AC until the start of its next turn, including against the triggering attack.

Actions
Multiattack: Ilvareth makes four Arcane Burst attacks.
Arcane Burst: Melee or Ranged Spell Attack: +9 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 150 ft., one target. Hit: 27 (4d10 + 5) force damage.
Spellcasting: Ilvareth casts one of the following spells, using Intelligence as the spellcasting ability (spell save DC 17): -

At will: detect magic, detect thoughts, disguise self, invisibility, light, mage armor, mage hand, prestidigitation

2/day each: fly, lightning bolt

1/day each: cone of cold, mind blank, scrying, teleport

```
Reed-Temple Warden
Medium humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

15

Hit Points: 

94 (21d8)

Speed: 

30 ft., climb 30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


10 (+0)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

14 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

Skills: 

Perception +5, Stealth +6

Saving Throws: 

DEX +6

Senses: 

passive Perception 15

Languages: 

Common plus one other language, Draconic

Challenge

5 (1,800 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Relentless Pursuit. While the reed-temple warden is moving toward a creature it has damaged within the last 24 hours, the reed-temple warden's speed can't be reduced and difficult terrain doesn't cost it extra movement.
Hold Breath. The reed-temple warden can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost the reed-temple warden extra movement, and the reed-temple warden can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Patient Defense. As a bonus action, the reed-temple warden takes the Dodge action.
Cunning Action. As a bonus action, the reed-temple warden takes the Dash, Disengage, or Hide action.

Actions
Multiattack: The reed-temple warden makes two attacks: one with its Unarmed Strike and one with its Dart. It can replace one attack with a use of Tail Sweep.
Unarmed Strike: Melee Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 23 (3d8 + 3) bludgeoning damage. Once on each of the reed-temple warden's turns when it hits with this attack, the target is subjected to the following effect. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Constitution saving throw or be stunned until the end of the reed-temple warden's next turn.
Dart: Ranged Weapon Attack: +6 to hit, range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 12 (2d8 + 3) piercing damage.
Tail Sweep: One creature within 5 feet of the reed-temple warden must succeed on a DC 14 Strength saving throw or be pushed up to 15 feet away from the reed-temple warden and knocked prone.
```

[concept] identity: A lizardfolk monk opens by reading the room — tail low, staff angled, circling to put an ally at its flank — then commits to a darting spring: a snapped kick or staff butt to the knee, a shove past reach, and a glide back into cover or shallow water before the return swing lands. It fights as a pack skirmisher, baiting pursuit so its kin can close from the blind side, and it does not break when bloodied: it goes quiet, slows the fight to a patient retreat-and-punish, and saves its one full-body lunge for the opponent who overextends.
[concept] lore: Born in marsh clans that taught stillness before striking, these scaled ascetics guard their reed-temples by letting raiders chase a single silent figure into the water and the waiting spears of their kin.

Pressed hard or wounded, it retreats down the shaft to its flooded hollow and waits, patient and silent, for another fool to lean over the rim.
[concept] lore: A drowned hunger that seeped into an abandoned well, it wears the water's surface like a face so that thirst will keep bringing it offerings.

CR 4 (XP 1,100; PB +2)

Traits

Crystal Plating. While Khordathar has at least 1 Hit Point, the razor-edged crystal plates harden Khordathar's hide, granting it a bonus to its Armor Class.

Fear Aura. Khordathar emanates an aura in a 20-foot Emanation while it doesn't have the Incapacitated condition. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 12, any enemy that starts its turn in the aura. Failure: The target has the Frightened condition until the start of its next turn. Success: The target is immune to this creature's aura for 24 hours.

Actions

Forelegs. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 17 (3d8 + 4) Slashing damage. If Khordathar moved at least 20 feet straight toward the target immediately before the hit and the target is Large or smaller, the target has the Prone condition, and Khordathar can make one Forelegs attack against it as a Bonus Action.

Chime Shriek (Recharge 5-6). Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 14, each creature in a 15-foot Cone. Failure: 24 (7d6) Piercing damage. Success: Half damage.

Armored Slam. Melee Attack Roll: +6, reach 5 ft. 17 (3d8 + 4) Bludgeoning damage, and the target has the Prone condition.

It herds its pale wingless kin across the corpse-soil like a shepherd of glass, raising its serrated forelegs and slamming its armored mass into anything that threatens the herd, then pins the intruder beneath its crystal belly to grind and stamp. It never flees and never toys — a wounded matriarch plants herself over the young and fights until the herd is clear or she cannot stand, screaming crystal-chime warnings across the field. It is fully sapient (INT 10-14), and it is a protective herd-beast that shields its own kind.

Meshrin
Large humanoid, neutral
Armor Class

16 (breastplate)

Hit Points: 

120 (14d10 + 42)

Speed: 

30 ft., swim 30 ft.

STR	DEX	CON	INT	WIS	CHA


18 (+4)

	

15 (+2)

	

16 (+3)

	

10 (+0)

	

12 (+1)

	

15 (+2)

DEX +5, WIS +4

passive Perception 14

Languages: 

Common, Draconic

Challenge

8 (3,900 XP)

Proficiency Bonus:

+3

Wounded Frenzy. While it has half of its hit points or fewer, Meshrin has advantage on attack rolls and saving throws.
Hold Breath. Meshrin can hold its breath for 15 minutes.
Great Weapon Master. When Meshrin hits a creature with its greataxe, the attack deals an extra 3 damage.
Marsh Stride. Difficult terrain composed of mud doesn't cost Meshrin extra movement, and Meshrin can move across mud without needing to make an ability check.
Binding Mark. As a bonus action, Meshrin marks one creature within 30 feet. That creature must succeed on a DC 15 Wisdom saving throw or, until the start of Meshrin's next turn, have disadvantage on attack rolls against creatures other than Meshrin.

Actions
Multiattack: Meshrin makes three attacks, using Greataxe or Javelin.
Greataxe: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) slashing damage.
Javelin: Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, range 30/120 ft., one target. Hit: 17 (3d8 + 4) piercing damage. Hit or miss, immediately after the attack, a javelin returns to Meshrin's grasp, and the target's speed is reduced by 10 feet until the start of Meshrin's next turn.

[concept] identity: A towering skeleton warrior strides forward with an oversized greatsword, opening with a sweeping overhead cleave meant to flatten the first thing in reach before the fight can begin. Round after round it plants itself and trades blows, its heavy blade working in patient, repeating arcs while its bony frame shrugs off cuts that would fell a living soldier. It has no blood to lose and no fear to feel, so it never breaks off or feints retreat; it simply advances until the blade stops moving.
[concept] lore: Raised from the grave-pits of an old battlefield where fallen champions were buried with their weapons, these skeletons rise whenever a greatsword remains unclaimed, cursed to guard the field their living selves once fought to win.

Riposte (Reaction). When a creature within 5 feet of Varro misses it with a melee attack roll, Varro makes one Handaxe attack against that creature.

Actions
Multiattack: Varro makes three Handaxe attacks. It can replace one attack with a use of Shortsword.
Handaxe: Melee or Ranged Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft. or range 20/60 ft., one target. Hit: 11 (2d6 + 4) slashing damage, and Varro has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shortsword: Melee Weapon Attack: +7 to hit, reach 5 ft., one target. Hit: 8 (1d8 + 4) piercing damage. Immediately after the attack, its handaxe returns to Varro's grasp, and Varro has advantage on its next attack roll against the target before the end of its next turn.
Shield Bash: One creature within 5 feet of Varro that it can see must make a DC 15 Strength saving throw, taking 9 (2d4 + 4) bludgeoning damage on a failed save. If the target is a Medium or smaller creature, it is also knocked prone.
```

[concept] identity: Brother Halric opens with a call to order — a barked command that turns a ragged warband into a fighting line — then wades in beside his troops, turning blades aside with a scarred iron-bound forearm while his iron-hafted mace cracks ribs and shatters knees. He plants himself at the line's weak point and will not yield ground; when bloodied he does not run, he rallies, fighting harder the fewer men he has left to lose.
[concept] lore: Brother Halric is a disgraced monastic sergeant who turned the abbey's rule of obedience into a doctrine of the field, gathering deserters and the desperate into a brotherhood that answers only his voice — he wants a disciplined band of his own, and he will break a village to get one.
